Title: Leales demostraciones, amantes finezas, y festivas aclamaciones de la novilissima ciudad de Manila, con que agradecida a los divinos beneficios expressa su fino amor en las nueve fiestas que celebró, patente el divino Rey de reyes en el SS. Sacramento, y colocada en la capilla mayor desta S. metropolitana iglesia la milagrosa imagen de María Santissima de Guía, en acción de gracias por el dichoso nacimiento de nuestro príncipe D. Luis Phelipe Fernando Joseph
Creator: the city of Manila (no personal author named on the title page); printed by Gaspar Aquino de Belén
Publisher: Manila: en la Imprenta de la Compañía de Jesús, por D. Gaspar Aquino de Belén, 1709
Publication Date: 1709
Rights: Public Domain (published 1709, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: spa
Source: Internet Archive item lealesdemostraci00aqui (https://archive.org/details/lealesdemostraci00aqui), the item's own lealesdemostraci00aqui_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Getty Research Institute; host metadata date: 1709. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 58c15eabbf5023cac7b2404718f59c9ecfb579a3121185f75c13934e19d8db6f.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Getty Research Institute scan; the preliminaries are garbled, the body readable but noisy, the long s read as f. The host lists Aquino de Belén as creator; the title page names him as printer only.
Content note: Manila's festival book for nine days of thanksgiving for the birth of the Spanish heir: the Blessed Sacrament exposed and the miraculous image of Our Lady of Guía carried into the main chapel of the cathedral, with the sermons preached on the feasts and the city's devotion to Guía as protector of the galleons.
